                                             # and Fedora machines.  For Ubuntu (which runs AppArmor), DevStack
                                             # already captures the contents of /var/log/kern.log (via

                                             fi

                                                 sudo gzip -r /var/core
                                                 sudo cp -r /var/core /home/zuul/
                                             fi
